This is crucial for live captioning during a phone call or meeting where even a slight delay is disruptive.
10-25 21:40:03.825 17048 17048 I native  : 2. **Instantaneous facial recognition for security:** Biometric authentication, such as unlocking a phone using a face scan, must happen locally for privacy and speed. 10-25 21:40:03.825 17048 17048 I native  : 3. **Offline language translation:** A phone can translate text or speech into another language even when there is no internet connection. This allows users to communicate in remote areas or during travel where cellular service is unavailable.
10-25 21:40:03.825 17048 17048 I native  : 4. **Immediate photo enhancement and editing:** AI-powered features like portrait mode depth mapping or real-time object removal can process images instantly on the device's GPU.
